## **146. Post by `@woody.fm` on 2026-09-15**

<div class="bsky-card" markdown="1">

<div class="bsky-content" markdown="1">

shopify.engineering/back-to-native One of the biggest software platforms in the world made an important decision Native apps are a better UX. LLMs makes them feasible. React Native means that platform evolution is always lagging (Liquid Glass) Every atproto project should do a similar eval.
